legacy parser print export download as pdf printable version in other projects wikimedia commons wikidata item appearance move to sidebar hide from wikipedia the free encyclopedia colombian latin pop group piso 21 background information origin medellin colombia genres latin pop reggaeton years active 2007 present labels warner latina tres sietes llc warner mexico warner colombia interscope members juan david huertas clavijo pablo mejia bermudez david escobar gallego past members david lorduy hernandez juan david m castano website piso 21 com piso 21 is a colombian latin pop group their singles me llamas besandote and dejala que vuelva were hits in their home country latin america and spain career edit piso 21 is a colombian group composed of pablo mejia pablito juan david huertas el profe and david escobar dim with colombian artists juan david castano el llane and david lorduy hernandez lorduy having been members of the group in past throughout two different time durations the name piso 21 derives from an elevator in a building in medellin colombia which had 21 floors 1 the group has toured extensively in south america and overseas 2 2007 2013 formation and debut edit the group formed in 2007 but it was not until 2012 that the group started receiving widespread recognition 3 with songs like vagones vacios angel mortal and correr el riesgo the group s fame in colombia steadily grew later in 2012 thanks to the success of their debut studio album piso 21 the group was nominated for best new artist in the latin grammy awards that same year 4 2014 2015 transition to urban music edit in 2014 piso 21 released the single suele suceder featuring puerto rican singer nicky jam the song was a commercial success in latin america and reached the top 20 in the colombian monitor latino charts 5 the song also became the group s first charting single in the us charts built for latin audiences by billboard where it peaked at number 29 on the tropical songs chart 6 the following year in 2015 the group released the singles quitate la pena delicia and hoy featuring chilean singer florencia arenas 2016 2018 warner music latina and ubuntu edit in 2016 piso 21 signed with warner music latina and later on in the year released the single me llamas which ended up reaching the top 40 in several countries throughout latin america and became their most successful single to date at the time 7 on 2 december 2016 an official remix featuring colombian singer maluma was released 8 the remix helped further add to the song s momentum in 2016 they became one of the most popular group in mexico having 4 concerts fully sold out while they love singing and playing in mexico they also enjoy spending time walking around the city and getting to know beautiful places around it an advantage that they have in mexico and that they ve always said is that they have their clothing designers who make their outfits for their concerts they love talking about rafael isaac in early 2017 they released the single besandote which like their previous single ended up becoming a widespread commercial success reaching the top 40 in several countries in latin america on 1 september 2017 the group released an official remix featuring british singer anne marie to celebrate their youtube channel surpassing over a million subscribers 9 on 19 october 2017 they released the single dejala que vuelva featuring manuel turizo 10 the song became an instant success across latin america where it topped multiple spotify charts in the region and became certified gold in colombia just merely two weeks after its release on 10 november 2017 they released the single tu heroe 11 which later ended up peaking at number seven on the paraguayan monitor latino charts 12 the song was then followed by the single adrenalina on 1 december 2017 which featured spanish rapper maikel delacalle 13 in early 2018 piso 21 collaborated with argentinian singer paulo londra on the single te amo which was released on 15 march 2018 14 on 11 may 2018 the group released the single la vida sin ti to coincide with the release of their second studio album ubuntu 15 later in the year on 14 september 2018 they released the song punto suspensivos as the eighth single from their second studio album ubuntu 16 the group then released the single te vi with venezuelan singer micro tdh on 14 december 2018 17 2019 2024 lorduy era edit in early 2019 on the 3 february one of the founding members of the group juan david castano el llane announced his departure from the group to pursue a career as a solo artist 18 he was subsequently replaced by the up and coming colombian artist david lorduy hernandez lorduy 19 20 in april 2020 the group released the compilation album canciones que nos marcaron featuring ten songs from their early years including suele suceder quitate la pena and delicia 21 in march 2021 the group released their fourth album el amor en los tiempos del perreo whose title references gabriel garcia marquez s novel love in the time of cholera 22 the album compiled singles released since 2019 including una vida para recordar with myke towers mami with black eyed peas pa olvidarme de ella with christian nodal dulcecitos with zion lennox and mas de la una with maluma 22 on october 13 2022 piso 21 released their fifth album 777 although relatively new this album is just as popular as their others the 16 track album featured collaborations with manuel turizo carin leon danny ocean nejo santa fe klan totoy el frio and gigolo y la exce 23 and entered the top 10 of spotify s global album debut chart 24 in september 2023 the group made its first tour of the united states the los muchachos tour with dates in new york city miami orlando houston and dallas 24 on 9 november 2023 they released los muchachos featuring nicky jam maluma ryan castro gabito ballesteros elena rose tezzel and fainal shako 25 in october 2024 they released 2 1 a 14 track album recorded over the course of a year in colombia and the united states with guest appearances by ozuna and wisin it was accompanied by el camino al 2 1 a 27 minute documentary short about the making of the record 26 its single la mision with wisin reached number one on the billboard latin pop airplay chart in 2024 27 2025 2026 trio and trescender edit in january 2025 lorduy announced his departure from the group 28 and the remaining members confirmed they would continue as a trio without a replacement 29 on 6 february 2025 they released volver with marc anthony and beele their first release as a trio 30 the song won best pop rhythmic song at the 2025 premios juventud 31 and was nominated for song composition of the year at the 2026 premios nuestra tierra 32 in july 2025 the group performed mas casa mas fama the official theme song of the third season of la casa de los famosos mexico 33 ahead of their next album they released the singles como el sol with bacilos october 2025 34 por si manana no estoy a bolero with andres cepeda december 2025 35 te elijo with yami safdie february 2026 36 and nunca me lo espere with lasso march 2026 37 on 12 march 2026 piso 21 released trescender their eighth album and their first as a trio 38 the title blends tres three for the number of members with trascender to transcend the 12 track album features marc anthony beele lasso yami safdie juan duque bacilos fonseca and andres cepeda 37 and was the group s first release on its own label tres sietes distributed by warner music mexico 39 27 2026 present interscope records edit on 14 september 2026 piso 21 signed a global distribution deal with interscope records under which the group continues to release music through tres sietes 27 on 18 september 2026 they released segunda cita with micro tdh and gangsta a follow up to their 2018 collaboration te vi which surpassed one billion streams on spotify 40 41 42 discography edit albums edit studio albums edit list of studio albums with selected details chart
